		function t237:_OnPlayed(p275)

				return

			local RollOffMaxDistance2 = p275.RollOffMaxDistance
			if 0 < RollOffMaxDistance2 and RollOffMaxDistance2 < Magnitude20 then
				return
			end
			local SoundVisualizer2 = v397.data.SoundVisualizer
			if 0 < SoundVisualizer2.MinVolume and p275.Volume < SoundVisualizer2.MinVolume then
				return
			end
			local FindFirstAncestorOfClass4 = t249:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
			if not self:_PassesSourceFilter(SoundVisualizer2.Source, FindFirstAncestorOfClass4) then
				return
			end

			local v443 = math.clamp(p275.Volume * v442, 0, 1)

			v446 = not (FindFirstAncestorOfClass4 == nil)
			v446 = v446 and f10047(FindFirstAncestorOfClass4) or {}

			end

				return
			end

			local v448 = t250.UseLoudnessColor
			v448 = v448 and f10050(v443) or t250.Color

		end
